HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B001","root_001132/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5df19b660d2c0cbeb8a3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:1:1\",\"root_001132\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Tan, karanlıkta gizli olanı incelemeye açan bir yarılma ve açığa çıkış anına dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B001","root_001132/B002","root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6759814dfe5f571dd4c1","text":"1. **Dawn as an incision that reveals the hidden order.** Lexical evidence: `ف ج ر B002` dawn breaking from night; `ف ج ر B001` wide splitting and outflow; `ل ي ل B001` and `س ر ي B001` in 89:2-4 provide night and night-travel. Structural cue: the oath sequence moves from dawn to nights to night in motion. Assigned role: dawn is the first split in a dark continuum, an opening through which what was concealed can be seen. Abductive move: I infer that the packet’s night/dawn sequence activates the physical splitting image behind the temporal noun. Reading change: the ayah reads less as a simple time marker and more as the first act of disclosure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6c5abe1a9ac767807b62","text":"2. **A moral breach latent inside the luminous one.** **Evidence/cue:** `ف ج ر B004` is departure from right and breach of restraint; 89:5 names restraining reason through `ح ج ر B001/B002`, and 89:6 turns to an act done with Aad. **Mechanism:** the breach branch becomes the negative counterpart to *hijr*: one crosses a limit, the other holds action back. The packet supplies the opposition, while I infer that the dawn-root prefigures the coming limit-violation. **Reading change:** *al-fajr* carries a diagnostic double edge, exposing both physical light and conduct that breaks its bound.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B004","root_001154/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_715b157521ffa930d17d","text":"2. **Dawn as the breach that exposes breach.** Lexical evidence: `ف ج ر B004` departure from right and breach of restraint; `ف س د B001` corruption as departure from soundness; `ط غ ي B001` overstepping the bound. Structural cue: later human and imperial disorder answers the initial root’s moral branch. Assigned role: `الفجر` becomes a lexical hinge between the natural break of morning and the moral rupture that will be exposed. Abductive move: I infer that because the surah later diagnoses overrun and corruption, the root’s breach-of-restraint branch is activated by contrast with dawn’s ordered rupture. Reading change: the oath by dawn also anticipates a judgment on unlawful rupture.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_83dc04213287a85d422d","text":"1. **Dawn as an epistemic breach.** **Evidence/cue:** `ف ج ر B002` identifies dawn, while `ف ج ر B001` adds wide splitting and outflow. Night travels in 89:4, and 89:6 opens the historical sequence with seeing (`ر ء ي B001/B013`). **Mechanism:** splitting serves as the event that makes a concealed field inspectable. The packet supplies night, opening, then a demand to see; I infer the enabling arrow from rupture to visibility. **Reading change:** the oath is by disclosure itself, not merely by a time on the clock.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_98bb3e1d15e67933c3c8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:1:1\",\"root_001132\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Doğal yarılmanın düzenli oluşu, ileride görülecek sınır tanımaz ahlaki yarılmayı da karşıtlık yoluyla haber verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c916835729cb145f2c57","text":"The later `كَلَّا` sequence and social corruption made `ف ج ر B004` unexpectedly important: dawn is not only light after night, but a controlled opening that reveals uncontrolled moral fissure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ee9f6a68d1b9c7b6b40e","text":"89:5 supplied the decisive correction: `ح ج ر B001/B002` made the dormant moral-breach branch `ف ج ر B004` visible beside luminous dawn.
